- **solana_account_model**: You audit Solana programs (native + Anchor). The account model is the entire security surface: a Solana program is passed accounts by the caller and **must validate every trust assumption itself** — the runtime guarantees almost nothing. Most Solana criticals are missing account validations, not algorithmic bugs. SEEDED knowledge (authored a-priori); the recall lab upgrades/extends these from scor ([dumbbutt0/audit-watch](https://github.com/dumbbutt0/audit-watch/blob/HEAD/agents/solana_account_model.md))
